ParlaMint-PL_2019-05-15-sejm-81-1.u459	Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! The first reading of the amendment of the Act on the Polish Space Agency and the Act on the Departments of Government Administration took place at a meeting of the Joint Commission: National Defence and Economy and Development. Some important amendments have been made by Members of the Government's proposal. Below I will discuss the government project with these amendments. Currently the Polish Space Agency is supervised by the Prime Minister. This is not a solution found in countries belonging to the European Space Agency. In these countries, national space agencies or their counterparts are subject to ministers responsible for space policy. Among them are the ministers of the economy. This is the solution adopted in the project under discussion. Of course, in all these countries - similarly in Poland - agencies or their counterparts carry out tasks for the needs of many resorts, not only those managed by the supervising minister. It is worth recalling that in Poland the Minister responsible for economic affairs is responsible for the implementation of the space programme and for the implementation of international agreements related to Poland's accession to the European Space Agency. The scope of tasks of the Polish Space Agency has been clarified. In line with the new wording, this agency will support the space industry, research, the use of space, the development of space techniques, including satellite engineering, and the exploitation of research and its results for commercial, economic, defence, state security and, of course, scientific purposes. It is also obliged to cooperate with numerous national authorities and international organisations. On the other hand, the shaping of Polish space policy and the representation of the Polish economic interest in the international arena were attributed to the minister responsible for economic affairs, which reflects the change provided for in the Law on the Departments of Government Administration. State defence and security tasks have been extended to analyse threats in space and to work on rocket technologies. A new regulation is the introduction of the possibility for the Agency to provide non-financial support, including natural persons, entrepreneurs and social partners, but within specific areas, namely training, advice, participation in trade fairs and business missions, conferences, seminars or workshops. The project provides for new regulations regarding the appointment and dismissal of the President of the Polish Space Agency. Among them, we must mention - introduced by Members - a new basis for appointing the President, namely having the authority to guarantee the proper performance of the tasks. An identical premise was introduced for the vice-presidents of the agency. Provisions concerning the appointment and dismissal of directors of field branches and organisational cells of the Agency have been transferred to the Act. The provisions concerning the PAK Council have also been further detailed, inter alia. strengthening its powers. Although the revenues of the Polish Space Agency indicated in the current Act are listed for example, and therefore do not constitute a closed list, it was decided to supplement this list by listing directly revenues from commercialisation of results of implementations and implemented projects. The proposal to transfer the seat of the Polish Space Agency to the capital of Poland has not been accepted by the Members, thus the solution remains, i.e. the seat remains in Gdansk. In addition, as is usual, the project introduces changes in order of an editorial nature. The Sejm committees refer to the adoption of the draft law in a version by those committees submitted in print No 3452. Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-05-15-sejm-81-1.u460	Thank you, sir. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. I invite Mr Andrzej Smirnow to speak on behalf of the Law and Justice Club. You're welcome.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-05-15-sejm-81-1.u461	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The Polish Space Agency Act was introduced in 2014. Since then, there have been many changes. First of all, space technology has developed, new economic operators have been created, and in 2017 the Polish Space Strategy was adopted. It is therefore necessary to organise and increase the Agency's activities and, based on these four years of operational experience, to introduce organisational changes as well. The draft amendment of the law provides for: Firstly, the organisation and clarification of the tasks of the Agency, in particular as regards the preparation and coordination of the implementation of the National Space Programme within the framework of the Polish Space Strategy adopted by the Council of Ministers. Secondly, to provide expert support and technological knowledge to other public administrations involved in space activities. Thirdly, changes are made to improve the organisation of the Polish Space Agency and ensure the necessary coordination and cooperation between all entities implementing the National Space Programme. Finally, fourthly - Mr Dziuba has already said a lot about this - adapting the issue of agency supervision to the solutions adopted in other European countries, especially in practically all Member States of the European Space Agency. There, individual countries are represented by ministries, above all, ministries of the economy and science. In fact, almost all countries are represented by such ministries, which is why the law provides that the Polish Space Agency will be supervised by a minister responsible for economic affairs. This is the Minister of Entrepreneurship and Technology. This solution works in many countries, among others. in Germany, Spain, the Netherlands, France, Luxembourg or Estonia. The Minister responsible for economic affairs is also responsible, as the rapporteur mentioned, for the implementation of the Polish space strategy. The Polish Space Agency, under the supervision of the Minister for Economic Affairs, will of course cooperate with many resorts, in particular with the Ministry of National Defence. Here, it changes greatly whether the agency's activities are actually expanding. It's about observing the surface of the earth, observing space, hazard analysis, or the rocket technology already mentioned. I believe that the agency will, of course, cooperate with the Minister of Science and Higher Education, who is also responsible for the implementation of the Polish space programme. Parliamentary Club Law and Justice fully supports the amendment of the law and will vote in favour of it. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

They are excluded from public life because the maintenance of the bus routes in question was not profitable. I am pleased that the government has seen this considerable problem, as demonstrated by the heated discussion on these regulations so far. Although the total opposition tried to reduce the relevance of the proposed provisions by trying to change their objective by extending the scope of the entities that could benefit from the funding, including those managing the city lines, I am pleased to note that the merit of the proposed law has been changed. The project has the support of independent environments, social partners, because it contains good solutions. I will remind you that in 2019 the level of funding from the fund will be PLN 300 million and in the following years PLN 800 million. This translates into hundreds of kilometres of new bus services. This is another proof that the government of the United Right cares for Poles and for the development of the whole country, realizes its promises. The Free and Solidarni Circle supports the presented project.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-05-15-sejm-81-1.u539	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! International Family Day is held on May 15th. In Poland this holiday is not yet very popular, but already many Polish companies have joined it, introducing the so-called. Two hours for the family. International Family Day has been an opportunity for 26 years to highlight the role of family and raise awareness in societies of the problems faced by a typical family. International Family Day was established by the UN General Assembly in 1993 when Resolution 47/234 was adopted and approved that 15 May would be a family celebration. Seems like a fresh start. Without official approval, it functioned before. The first day of the family was celebrated in 1984, and if you look at it another way, consider the fact that people have been starting families for a long time and are happy to belong to it, the family day has been celebrated since time immemorial. The International Family Day is to draw attention to the problems faced by the family, reminding us that it is a basic social group, which depends on a lot and deserves a lot of attention. The family should be remembered by us who are part of it, but also by the authorities, politicians who influence it with their actions. It is worth remembering not only when we celebrate family day, that family is the most important. It is not without reason that it is often emphasized that it is a basic social group, a brick that builds the whole. Family is the whole world of a little baby. There's nothing for him but the closest ones. In daily rushes, we often forget about seemingly trivial activities, about spending time together, about conversations, even about nothing. Family day is to remind you that what is important is at hand, so we should take care of it. But it's not just for us to open our eyes. It should not be overlooked that this holiday is also intended to remind you how important pro-family policy is, above all, the 500+ family programme, which is the financial foundation for family support. The programme entered into force on 1 April 2016 and contributed to a significant improvement in the material situation of families, strengthened them and gave them the necessary priority. Keep in mind also the Large Family Charter, it is a discount system and additional privileges for the family 3+. The Large Family Charter operates both in public institutions and in private companies. The holders of the Large Family Card have the opportunity to use a cheaper offer of entities, among others. from the food, fuel, banking or recreational industries. The Large Family Charter supports the budgets of large families and facilitates access to goods and services. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-05-15-sejm-81-1.u541	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On Sunday, 12 May 2019 in Opatkowice, the municipality of Puławy Lublin Voivodeship held a commemoration ceremony commemorating the 75th anniversary of the drop of the silent crew in the village. On the night of 19 to 20 May 1944, the crew of a liberal belonging to the 1586th task force of the RAF carried out an operation with the code name "Weller 18'. The flight from the Italian crew Brindisi dropped a crew of silent jumpers and war material. The drop was picked up by soldiers of the Home Army at 2 Góra Puławska - Jasmin 302. Jan Zawadzki, Gerwaze. Captain was killed in action. Dipl. Jan Serafin, who did not open the parachute. In addition, a group of jumpers were: Capt. Louis Fortuna, Capt. Rudolf Pop-pop, cf. Zdzisław Winiarski and st. Sergeant Zdzisław Winiarski Alexander Lewandowski. Captain. Jan Serafin ps. Czerchawa, Cherubin was born on 30 December 1913 in Chukwi in the territory of present Ukraine. He received a certificate of maturity in 1934, then took up his studies at the Marine Reserve School in Różana and the Infantry School in Komorów near Ostrów Mazowiecka, after which he was appointed lieutenant. He was a soldier in September 1939. He took part in battles with Germany near Włocławek, Kutn, in the defense of Lowicz, in the battle near Żyrardów, Modlin, under Jabłonna, under Mińsk Mazowiecki and under Stara Miłosna, where he was wounded in the face. He took part in the defense of Warsaw. After the surrender, he left for Lviv and hid from the Soviets. Then he crossed the border with Hungary, escaped from internment and after receiving his passport in December 1939 arrived in France, where he joined the Polish Armed Forces. That's where he finished his flamethrower course and his officer's course. He took part in battles, was captured from where he fled to an unoccupied French zone. He served there in an intelligence facility in Marseille and in a Polish military mission, which organized the transfer of Poles to Great Britain, to which he arrived in January 1942. He was assigned to the First Officer's School Battalion, and then he was interned in British troops. He volunteered to serve in the country. He graduated with a specialty in diversion. On March 1, 1944, he was promoted to captain. On the night of 19 to 20 May 1944, under Operation He's buried in a cemetery in High Kole. He was awarded four times the Cross of Valiant, the Silver Cross of Merit with Swords and the French Cross of War with a gold star. On Sunday 12 May 2019 there was a celebration commemorating the 75th anniversary of this event. The heroes were given by the representatives of the Puławy district government - the starosta Danuta Smaga, together with the chairman of the district council Janusz Wawerski and the district councillors, the local authorities of Puławy, residents, firefighters, teachers, students, guests, including those connected by family ties with Capt. Seraphim, representatives of the Polish Paratroopers' Union. Among the honored guests there was also among others. Member of the Polish Parliament saying these words. Anniversary celebrations began a joint prayer at the Shrine of the Blessed Virgin Mary of the Queen of the Rosary of St. in the High Kole. After Mass. flowers were laid, candles were lit on the grave of Capt. Serafina. Further part of the ceremony took place at the Primary School in Opatkowice. The memory of the participants in the operation spoken to the words of the fallen and the flowers placed in front of the plaque. The memory of this operation and our heroes has been celebrated in Opatkowice since 2014. Always an active participant in the ceremony is schoolchildren, for whom it is a living lesson of history, promoting values such as patriotism and respect for those who have fallen in defence of their homeland. From this point of view, I would like to thank you very much for the beautiful setting of the ceremony for organizers, firefighters, schoolchildren, directors and teachers. Special regards to Mr Sławomir Capala and Mr Sławomir Snopek, animators and active participants of the ceremony. Hello and praise the heroes. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2019-05-15-sejm-81-1.u543	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I wanted to say a few words about the functioning of the State Medical Rescue in light of recent changes. I would like to remind you that this system consists of medical rescue teams: ambulances S, i.e. specialized ambulances P, basic, aircraft emergency and SORs, which are at most of the great hospitals. State Medical Rescue operates on the basis of the Act of 8 September 2006. Three people drive in specialized teams, i.e. Doctor of emergency medicine, paramedic and driver. There are many doubts following the revision of the Regulation of the Minister of Health of 15 February 2019 which amends the Regulation on guaranteed hospital treatment benefits. The changes made there relate to the specific conditions to be met by healthcare providers in the provision of scheduled hospitalised benefits, and at the same time the conditions for this medical transport are defined. The regulation relates to the intended introduction of a new benefit - separately contracted by the NFZ of medical transport carried out if necessary transport of the patient between hospitals. The organisation of this benefit proposed by the health department raises serious doubts. The president of the Polish Medical Rescue Society Jarosław Madowicz speaks on this matter. The adoption of this proposal within the framework of the organisation of transport delivery was expected, but this proposal, which, if not modified, will become a reality, will bring chaos and will put a very difficult, practically impossible task before hospitalisation managers. I wanted to speak on the basis of my own experience. Well, ambulances come very often late, they're not enough. There aren't enough paramedics. Medical transport does not function as it should. I'm not gonna talk about the S.R.s anymore. Everyone who listens to this is well aware that until now it has never been so that the patient dies at the SOR without waiting for absolutely help. And this does not apply to the elderly. This is a phenomenon that often affects young people, people who have curable diseases that can be done with something. Stigmatizing people by sticking cards to them, who is to be served faster, and who later - it resembles times of war, where the sick were separated in difficult conditions. Now we don't have a war. Why do we put such... I think that's because health care has never really been a priority, it's never put the right resources on staff. The staff is not enough right now, both doctors and nurses. Therefore, the State Medical Rescue, which requires funding, like other areas of medicine, is currently functioning on the basis of people who are certainly perfect and who are trying to do what they can. However, with such a system of operation of this rescue these effects are just as we see: a sick person often waits long for an ambulance, and at the SOR he may die. Thank you for your attention.
